`activate_concierge` server-action update to use the LEAST/GREATEST formula; new `recompute_concierge_expiry(event_id)` server action wired to the `events.wedding_date` update trigger OR app-layer hook in the Concierge Step 1 + Profile edit flows; advisory in-app + email notification template (per 0028); UI advisory banner in dashboard + settings + Concierge Step 1 form. **Sequencing note:** Third 2026-05-17 row · refines the access-duration semantics of the second 2026-05-17 row (single-SKU + 3-day trial + tiered enforcement) without changing the price, the trial mechanics, or the enforcement framework · `wedding` remains the only V1-selectable event type · V1 apply-then-pay discipline preserved · no new SKU added (formula change only on the existing `concierge_complete` SKU). |
| 2026-05-17 | **Setnayan Pay convenience fee minimum floor locked at ₱50 — extends the flat 5.0% lock with a per-booking floor.** Formula at checkout: `fees_centavos = MAX(subtotal_centavos × setnayan_fee_bps / 10000, min_fee_centavos)` where `min_fee_centavos = 5000` (₱50) is the default in `payment_method_config` per 0023 § 3.5d (admin-configurable per method · single-admin-authority + audit-log pattern matches the rate column). **Activates only below ₱1,000 vendor booking** (the 5%-meets-₱50 crossover); 99%+ of wedding-vendor bookings are unaffected. Affects mostly small supply purchases / micro add-ons in the supplies marketplace. **Customer-facing copy** (cart drawer · marketing site · vendor agreement): *"Setnayan Pay convenience fee — 5% per booking, ₱50 minimum."* **Vendor opt-in interaction (per 0034 § 6.8):** if vendor absorbs the convenience fee on a small booking, ₱50 floor still applies to vendor — floor protects Setnayan's per-transaction operating cost regardless of which side pays. **Worked example at the floor:** ₱500 vendor item → ₱50 floor (5% × ₱500 = ₱25, floor wins) → customer pays ₱550 total. Crossover at exactly ₱1,000 booking. **Schema migration** (V1 deployable): `ALTER TABLE payment_method_config ADD COLUMN min_fee_centavos INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 5000`. | Owner pick: ₱50 over ₱150/₱200 alternatives I proposed. Economic trade-off accepted: ₱50 × 72% (V1 28% tax wedge) = ₱36 net per small booking — below the V1 ₱80-130 operating cost band (admin time + disbursement absorption), so Setnayan loses ~₱50-90 on each booking below ₱1,000 in V1. But sub-₱1K vendor bookings are rare in the wedding category (1-2% of all bookings; supplies/micro-add-ons only); aggregate annual loss is ~₱720 at 200 couples → ~₱3,600 at 1,000 couples — negligible against platform revenue (rounding error against ~₱14M+ at 1K-couple scale). V1.5+ rebalances cleanly: Maya Bulk Fund Transfer drops admin cost to ₱30-50, so ₱36 net at the floor moves to roughly breakeven. ₱50 also anchors close to PH payment-processor flat-fee norms (PayMongo ₱5-15 · Stripe ~₱17 equivalent · GCash transfer ₱15) — feels fair to customers; the ₱150 alternative I proposed would have felt punitive on small purchases ("30% effective on a ₱500 booking" reads worse than "10% on a ₱500 booking" even though both clear Setnayan's break-even at scale). **Customer-experience priority** over per-booking margin discipline on the rare small-booking case. | `0034_payments_and_cart/0034_payments_and_cart.md` § 6.1 cart math — added Minimum fee floor paragraph + worked example for a ₱500 booking at the floor + crossover-point note + vendor opt-in interaction + customer-facing copy block; § 3.3 checkout formula at line 480 updated to `MAX(subtotal_centavos × setnayan_fee_bps / 10000, min_fee_centavos)` with cross-reference to 0023 § 3.5d. `0023_admin_console/0023_admin_console.md` § 3.5d `payment_method_config` schema extended with `min_fee_centavos INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 5000` column (between `gateway_fee_bps` and `is_preferred_default`). `0034` in-file decision log new row appended. **`.docx` mirrors NOT regenerated** (pandoc unavailable per established convention). **Engineering implementation pending:** `ALTER TABLE payment_method_config ADD COLUMN min_fee_centavos INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 5000` migration · cart math at checkout updated to `MAX(...)` formula · cart drawer UI shows "₱50 minimum" suffix when floor is active · marketing-site copy updated (`/pricing` worked example) · vendor agreement updated to surface the floor. **Sequencing note:** extends the flat 5.0% inbound-fee lock (2026-05-16 evening row) with a minimum-fee floor primitive · admin-configurable so V1.5+ can tune per-method if needed · no V1 SKU/feature scope expansion (this is fee-math refinement, not customer-facing surface change) · zero impact on the still-deferred V1.5+ gateway-absorption Path A/B/C/D decision. |
| 2026-05-17 | **App Store-style add-on detail surface — Panood pilot shipped; shared layout will fan out to other 8 add-ons + vendor service detail.** Owner directive: when a couple opens an individual add-on from the Add-ons bottom-nav (Panood as the worked example), the detail screen should mirror the iOS App Store listing: hero with icon + tagline + price-from + Choose Plan CTA, horizontal stat carousel (rating · purchases · % events use it · price-from), Preview screenshot rail, Sample outputs rail, About-this-feature description + Plans & pricing table, Ratings & Reviews ▸ deep-link, Event Privacy, Data Linked to You, Accessibility. **Same template applies to vendor service detail** when a couple opens an individual service from a vendor's profile (planned fan-out after the Panood pilot validates). **Architecture decisions:** (1) The previous `panood/page.tsx` (986 lines of post-purchase OAuth + broadcaster setup) is the *owner* surface and moves to `panood/setup/page.tsx`; the new `panood/page.tsx` is the *discovery* surface; the App Store hero CTA flips by ownership — not-owned shows **Choose plan · from ₱499/day** opening a bottom-sheet (mobile) / right-drawer (desktop) of all 7 Panood SKUs, owned shows **Open Panood setup** linking to `/setup`. (2) Shared layout components live at `apps/web/app/_components/app-store/{layout.tsx, choose-plan-sheet.tsx}` — content-agnostic so vendor service detail can mount the same component with different data. (3) New `public.feature_reviews` table (sibling of `vendor_reviews`) — one row per (feature_key, event_id, couple_user_id), public read + couple-only write tied to `current_couple_event_ids()`; writes are gated post-event in the app layer; surfaced on `/dashboard/[eventId]/add-ons/panood/reviews`. (4) Stat carousel reads live from `orders` + `feature_reviews` + `events` via `apps/web/lib/add-on-stats.ts`; below thresholds (orders < 5 AND reviews < 3) the layout renders a "Just launched · early access" chip per the launch-honesty rule. (5) Plans table + Choose-plan sheet drive every row off the V1 SKU lock (`service_catalog`), formatted via `formatPhp()` from `lib/orders.ts` — PHP only, no token display (token wallet was retired 2026-05-11). Add-to-cart deep-links to `/dashboard/[eventId]/orders/new?service=<sku_code>` reusing the 0034 apply-then-pay flow. (6) Three SKU drifts logged for catch-up by the next Cowork pass: iteration `0011_panood.md` lists Same-Day Edit at **₱24,999** but `service_catalog` (V1 lock 2026-05-16) has it at **₱9,999** — page renders the catalog number (single source of truth) and the spec line is stale; iteration spec also lists `custom_monogram_pack` + `broadcast_style_pack` + `pro_camera_bridge_addon` SKUs that are absent from the current `service_catalog` seed (cut from V1 per the 2026-05-16 BYO-YouTube pivot but the spec body still mentions them in section bodies); iteration spec still has a `token_display: 60000` line in the wallet-integration block which is stale per the 2026-05-11 token-wallet retirement (no UI consequence — the page surfaces PHP only). | Three drivers. **First, the existing detail surface jumps straight to broadcaster setup,** which assumes the couple already owns Panood — there is no discovery layer for a couple who's never broadcast before. The App Store pattern is the well-known design language for "here's a thing you might buy" detail pages and lets us bring the comparison, the social proof, the privacy disclosures, and the plan picker onto a single coherent screen. **Second, the same template solves the vendor service detail problem cleanly** — when a couple opens an individual service from a vendor's profile, they need the same shape of info (preview screenshots, sample work, plans, reviews, privacy). One shared `<AppStoreLayout>` component means we ship the Panood pilot, then fan out to the other 8 add-ons + vendor service pages by writing content props rather than rebuilding. **Third, the live DB aggregates (no curated marketing numbers) plus the "Just launched" chip below thresholds honors the launch-honesty rule** the owner has been consistent about — we never inflate. The stat carousel automatically transitions from "Just launched" → real numbers once usage crosses 5 orders OR 3 reviews per feature, with zero engineering churn at the threshold. **Flipsides accepted:** the page initially shows mostly placeholder preview/sample tiles since Setnayan does not yet ship marketing screenshots for Panood (real assets drop into `public/add-ons/panood/preview/*` later — the layout already accepts ReactNode so a future Image swap is a one-line change per tile); | 2026-05-17 | **Event date-change flow with booked vendors — multi-party state machine locked.** Customer-facing date edits route through a confirmation gate that triggers only when the event has **≥1 confirmed vendor** (cart/draft don't count toward the threshold; mediation-state vendors do). The gate is a single modal with the affected-vendor list + a required reason field (min 30 chars). On submit: schedule check runs against each vendor's calendar; couple sees one of two messages — *"Your schedule change is available for all vendors. Waiting for their confirmation."* OR *"Your schedule change is available for X vendors but conflicts for Y vendors. We cannot apply this change until all vendors accept."* Pending state freezes everything (original date holds on both sides, no further date attempts by couple, payments paused, vendor slots locked from re-allocation). Vendor decline does NOT auto-fail the request — it routes the booking into the existing customer↔vendor chat thread (per the customer-initiates-chat rule), which gains a **structured action card** with Propose alternate / Accept this date / Cancel booking / Escalate to support buttons. Free-text agreements in chat are not load-bearing — only the action cards trigger state transitions, otherwise bookings sit frozen indefinitely. Timeouts both ways: vendor ghost past **48 hr** → auto-escalate to admin mediation tier (0023 Disputes Handler joins the thread, same pattern as the force-majeure flow in 0019 + the refund/dispute menu in iteration 0021 § 2.2); couple ghost past **72 hr** of active chat negotiation → request auto-cancels and event reverts to original date. Mediation outcomes (mediator selects from a fixed enum, no free-text resolutions): vendor reconsiders, couple stays on original date, vendor swap, full refund + cancellation of that vendor only, reschedule-fee compromise. Race condition: if a vendor backs out *during* an active date-change negotiation and that drops the confirmed-vendor count to zero, the in-flight negotiation auto-resolves and the couple is free to apply the change immediately. When the count transitions ≥1 → 0 for any reason (last vendor backs out, mediation cancels everyone, all vendors released), a passive "All vendors have been released — you can now change your event date freely" banner surfaces on the dashboard Home; dismisses on first date-edit attempt or after 14 days. Reschedule fees: vendors enter their per-window policy at onboarding (0006); V1 leaves fee enforcement as a manual mediator action (auto-debit lands in 0034 Phase 2). Hard cap of **2 date changes** per event lifecycle; third attempt is gated behind admin override via 0029 help center. Locked into iteration 0021 § 10 (new section). | Wedding-date changes are emotionally and contractually high-stakes — Filipino weddings involve venue deposits, vendor schedules booked 8–12 months out, and family travel logistics. A naive "edit and save" date field would either lock couples out of legitimate changes (force majeure, family emergency, venue issue) or break vendor trust by moving the date without their input. Routing both sides through structured negotiation surfaces (modal → schedule check → vendor confirm → chat with action cards → admin mediation) preserves vendor agency without leaving couples stranded, captures a reason for every change (audit trail + vendor empathy + admin dispute pattern detection), and reuses existing chat + force-majeure mediation infrastructure rather than building a parallel surface. Affects iterations: 0021 (couple dashboard — canonical spec, new § 10), 0006 (vendor side — receives the request action card + reschedule policy capture), 0019 (chat infrastructure — must support structured/interactive action cards as a first-class message type), 0023 (admin console — mediation tier + dispute ticket reuse), 0034 (payment freeze during pending state · auto-debit of reschedule fees deferred to Phase 2). |
| 2026-05-17 | **App Store hero-CTA 5-state model locked — Add · Request Sent · Launch · Blocked · Expired — plus event-switcher expired-rightmost sort.** Owner directive after the Panood pilot ship: replace the binary "Choose plan" / "Open setup" CTA with five explicit states the App Store-style hero button cycles through. **(1) Add** — no active order against any feature SKU; opens the Choose Plan sheet. **(2) Request Sent** — couple has submitted an order but admin hasn't confirmed payment (`orders.status ∈ {'submitted','awaiting_payment'}`); button is a chip-style link to `/dashboard/[eventId]/orders/[orderPublicId]` so the couple can see the reference code + payment status. **(3) Launch** — feature is paid/fulfilled (`orders.status ∈ {'paid','fulfilled'}`); button links to the per-feature setup surface (for Panood: `/setup`; for Mood Board: the feature itself; for vendor services: the booking workspace). **(4) Blocked** — admin policy disables the feature for this account type (or per-event override applies); button is a disabled chip with the admin block-reason as the tooltip. **(5) Expired** — event passed its **wedding_date + 90 day** grace cutoff (OR is admin-archived); button is a disabled chip explaining the event ended. Resolution precedence: **expired > blocked > launch > request_sent > add**. The hero `statusPill` mirrors the state (Active on this event / Pending review / Unavailable / Event ended / Web V1 fallback for add). The same model applies to vendor service detail pages — same resolver, accountType=vendor_certified for vendor-side rendering. **Event switcher sort change:** active events first (primary → date-asc, unchanged inside the active group), then expired events pushed to the end with **date-desc** so the oldest-expired event lands at the rightmost / final slot ("oldest expired is the farthest" per owner directive). Archived events stay filtered out of the switcher per existing behavior — V1 keeps that surface, but the expired-by-date path now sorts cleanly to the right without dropping events. **Schema:** new `public.feature_policy(feature_key PK, enabled_for_couples, enabled_for_vendors_coming_soon, enabled_for_vendors_certified, block_reason_* TEXT, updated_by_admin_id, created_at, updated_at)` + `public.event_feature_policy_override(event_id+feature_key PK, enabled, reason, set_by_admin_id, set_at)` — mirrors the per-account-type policy primitive locked earlier today for payment methods. RLS: public read on feature_policy (state must be readable to render the CTA); per-event override scoped to `current_event_ids()` for couples + open to admins. Admin-only write on both. Seed enables all 9 add-on launcher keys for couples; vendors_coming_soon gated off by default except `orders` (vendor side reads orders too). **No admin UI in this PR** — admins flip flags via direct DB until the 0023 Feature Policy panel ships in a fan-out PR. | Two drivers. **First, the binary owned/not-owned CTA hid three important states the couple needed to see.** Request Sent in particular was invisible — a couple who submitted an order via apply-then-pay would land back on the detail page with no signal that anything was in flight, which trains "I should re-submit" behavior that costs admins reconciliation time. Surfacing the reference code in the chip closes the loop. Blocked is the existing payment-options policy primitive applied to features — admins already have per-account-type levers for payment rails (locked earlier today); the same primitive now covers feature visibility so a vendor in coming_soon state doesn't get pitched Same-Day Edit and a couple under abuse review doesn't see a buyable Concierge tile. Expired protects everyone — a couple revisiting their dashboard 6 months post-wedding doesn't get a "buy more" CTA on a feature they can't use; they see an "Event ended" chip instead. **Second, the event-switcher expired-rightmost sort matches Filipino couple behavior with multiple weddings.** A couple who married + ran a sibling's wedding + is now planning a vow renewal sees three events; the active/in-flight one belongs first, the completed ones belong on the right, and oldest-completed belongs farthest right (out-of-the-way but still accessible for downloads/reviews/photos). The prior `event_date.localeCompare` sort buried recent + active events under chronologically-earlier (long-past) events; the new sort fixes that without adding any new UX surface. **Flipsides accepted:** the 90-day grace is a guess (no production data yet); if couples report wanting more time, the constant `EXPIRATION_GRACE_DAYS` is a one-line change. The feature_policy primitive ships without an admin UI, which means policy changes require direct DB until the 0023 panel ships — acceptable because the V1 seed is permissive (all add-ons enabled for couples) and the override table is empty at launch, so no production blocks exist out of the gate. | `supabase/migrations/20260517010000_feature_policy.sql` NEW — `feature_policy` table + `event_feature_policy_override` table + RLS (public read on policy · per-event override scoped to `current_event_ids()` for couples / open to admins · admin-only write on both) + seed for 9 add-on keys + `tg_feature_policy_set_updated_at` trigger. `apps/web/lib/add-on-state.ts` NEW — `resolveAddOnState(supabase, eventId, featureKey, accountType?, setupHref?)` returns `AddOnStateContext {state, href, pendingOrderPublicId, blockReason, expiredAt}` with the documented precedence rule; `isEventExpired(event)` helper exported for shared use. `apps/web/app/_components/app-store/state-cta.tsx` NEW — server component `<AddOnStateCta>` picks the right render per state (ChoosePlanSheet for add · Rocket-iconed Link for launch · amber-tone Clock3 chip for request_sent · muted Lock chip for blocked · muted CheckCircle2 chip for expired) + `statusPillForState()` helper so the hero pill stays in lock-step. `apps/web/app/dashboard/[eventId]/add-ons/panood/page.tsx` — swap from binary `eventOwnsFeature` to `resolveAddOnState`; rename triggerLabel "Choose plan" → "Add" per owner's preferred label; secondary "Broadcaster preview" CTA continues to surface only on the launch state. `apps/web/lib/events.ts` — new `EVENT_EXPIRATION_GRACE_DAYS = 90` const + `isEventExpiredForSwitcher()` helper + `sortEventsForSwitcher<T>(events)` that partitions active/expired and sorts each group per the directive. `apps/web/app/dashboard/layout.tsx` — call `sortEventsForSwitcher()` on the visible events array after filtering archived. **`.docx` mirrors NOT regenerated** (pandoc unavailable per established convention). **Engineering follow-up pending:** 0023 admin console grid for managing `feature_policy` (toggles per account-type · per-feature row · per-event override sub-table) — same UI pattern as the planned payment-options policy matrix; fan-out — wire `resolveAddOnState` + `<AddOnStateCta>` into the other 8 add-on detail pages once the Panood pilot validates user-side; vendor service detail pages adopt the same hook with `accountType='vendor_certified'`. **Sequencing note:** Sixth 2026-05-17 row · extends the App Store pilot from the previous row with the 5-state CTA model + event-switcher sort · `feature_policy` is V1-deployable now (read-only at launch via direct DB writes; admin grid follows in 0023) · zero V1 SKU/scope expansion (no new features; this is CTA-state plumbing on existing routes) · zero impact on the wedding-only V1 lock (`event_type='wedding'` unchanged) · zero impact on wallet retirement (no token surface introduced). |
| 2026-05-17 | **Event venue-change flow — reuses the date-change state machine with a coverage-check substitution.** Same multi-party pattern as the § 10 date-change flow (gate triggered by ≥1 confirmed vendor · required reason field · pending-state freeze across all surfaces including the public landing page · 48 hr vendor response window · 72 hr couple chat timeout · chat negotiation via structured action cards · admin mediation tier · 2-changes cap · door-reopened banner). Two substantive differences: **(1)** the per-vendor check is a **coverage check** (does this vendor's service area / equipment / permits / licensing extend to the new venue?) rather than a schedule check. Customer-facing messaging adapts accordingly: *"Your venue change is available for all vendors. Waiting for their confirmation."* OR *"Your venue change is available for X vendors but is in conflict for Y vendors. We cannot apply this change until all vendors accept."* **(2)** the per-vendor fee field is a **relocation fee** (travel cost · equipment shipping · venue-specific insurance riders) rather than a reschedule fee, surfaced from vendor onboarding (0006) the same way. **Auto-accept policy (NEW · venue-only):** vendors set a service-area radius at onboarding (e.g., "auto-accept any venue within 50 km of Manila"); venues inside the radius skip the vendor confirmation card entirely and the booking auto-transitions. Venues outside the radius (or vendors who opted out of auto-accept) get the standard card. Cuts negotiation volume materially for short-distance venue swaps (e.g., changing from one Tagaytay venue to another). Mediation outcome enum mirrors § 10.6 with venue-specific labels: vendor reconsiders → service new venue · couple stays at original venue · vendor swap · refund + cancellation · relocation-fee compromise. Locked into iteration 0021 § 11 (new section). | Filipino weddings frequently change venue late in planning — venue contracts can fall through (overbooking, lost license, structural issue, ownership change); couples relocate for typhoon season; family politics intervene; budget reallocation between venue + catering. A naive "edit and save" venue field strands vendors who can't service the new location (e.g., catering vendor with no permit for the new municipality, lights & sound with travel constraints, florist outside service area). Reusing the § 10 state machine with a coverage-check substitution keeps engineering scope tight (same UI · same state transitions · same admin tooling) — only the vendor-side check differs. Auto-accept radius cuts the operational tax on the common case (short-distance moves) where vendor disruption is minimal. Affects iterations: 0021 (canonical spec, new § 11), 0006 (vendor side — service-area radius + auto-accept policy at onboarding · relocation fee policy capture), 0019 (chat infrastructure — same structured-card dependency as § 10), 0023 (admin mediation tier — venue-specific outcome labels), 0034 (relocation-fee debit via Phase 2). |
| 2026-05-17 | **Event guest-count change flow — monotonic ratchet with vendor confirmation on increases, decreases blocked outright.** Guest count is a numeric attribute, not a swap, so the state machine diverges from § 10 / § 11 in two material ways: **(1)** changes are **direction-restricted** — couples can increase the planning guest count but cannot decrease it once any guest-count-dependent vendor has confirmed at that count. The minus stepper on the guest-count field is disabled at the UI layer with hint *"Cannot decrease below confirmed floor — vendors have already committed to N guests"* (mirrors the disabled-CTA pattern from § 2.2d.i self-review block). **(2)** the change touches only **guest-count-dependent vendors** — a per-vendor flag (`is_guest_count_dependent`) captured at onboarding (0006) marks which vendors plan against the count: defaults TRUE for catering, florals, mobile bar, lights & sound, name cards, favors, printable invites; defaults FALSE for photography, videography, HMUA, planner, broadcast. Only dependent vendors receive the confirmation card and only their capacity / pricing recalculates. **Ratchet enforcement:** floor = MAX(all confirmed counts ever in this event's history). New vendors booked later inherit the current floor as their starting commitment. Vendor back-out dissolves THAT vendor's commitment but the floor for remaining dependent vendors stays — couples cannot reduce because other vendors are still planning at the higher count. **Confirmation gate (only on increase):** modal surfaces per-vendor cost impact (e.g., "+20 plates × ₱1,200 = +₱24,000 for Casa Manila Catering") and total delta before vendor cards fire. **Per-vendor check is a capacity check** — does this vendor have the headcount capacity at the current proximity to event? Customer-facing messaging: *"Your guest count change is available for all vendors. Waiting for their confirmation."* OR *"Your guest count change is available for X vendors but exceeds capacity for Y vendors. We cannot apply this change until all vendors accept."* Same chat negotiation / mediation tier / 48 hr · 72 hr timeouts / race condition handling as § 10. **Decrease override:** couples experiencing genuine catastrophic cancellation (force majeure, mass family withdrawal) can request a floor reduction via 0029 help center → admin mediator works with each affected vendor case-by-case to negotiate downward; default behavior remains decrease-blocked. Locked into iteration 0021 § 12 (new section). | Caterers, florists, printers, and mobile-bar operators commit inventory + crew based on the guest count: ingredients pre-ordered, centerpieces fabricated, name cards printed, crew rostered. A late decrease causes irrecoverable financial loss for the vendor (perishable inventory + non-refundable supplier deposits) without a corresponding refund for the couple. The ratchet protects vendor margin while preserving couple flexibility on the only direction that's still possible (upward — vendors can scale up by sourcing more inventory). Direction-restriction is enforced at the UI rather than negotiated through the state machine because it's not a "vendor preference" issue — it's a physical commitment-of-inventory issue that mediation cannot reverse. The admin-override path covers genuine catastrophes where the couple has no alternative. Affects iterations: 0021 (canonical spec, new § 12), 0006 (vendor side — `is_guest_count_dependent` flag + per-head rate · capacity ceiling at proximity-to-event · capacity confirmation card), 0019 (chat infrastructure — same structured-card dependency as § 10), 0023 (admin mediation tier — decrease-override workflow), 0034 (per-head delta charges → existing milestone-payment flow). |
| 2026-05-17 | **Per-vendor change-acceptance cutoffs — vendors set three cutoff windows (date / venue / guest count) at onboarding; if any confirmed vendor is past their cutoff for the change type, the standard change flow is blocked outright and routes to admin override.** Each vendor record carries three fields captured at onboarding (0006): `date_change_cutoff_days_before_event`, `venue_change_cutoff_days_before_event`, `guest_count_change_cutoff_days_before_event` (NULL when vendor isn't dependent on the attribute). **V1 ships with conservative category-based defaults pre-filled at vendor account creation** — vendors edit in 0022 Settings: catering 30/21/14 days · florals 21/14/10 · mobile bar 21/14/7 · lights & sound 14/21/7 · photography 7/3/NULL · videography 7/3/NULL · HMUA 7/3/NULL · planner 7/3/7 · live stream / broadcast 3/3/NULL · name cards/favors/print 21/14/10. **Pre-flight gate on every change attempt** (date / venue / guest count): before the § 10.1 / § 11.1 / § 12.3 confirmation modal fires, the system checks each confirmed vendor's cutoff for the change type vs current proximity to event date. If any vendor is past their cutoff, the standard flow is blocked with a dedicated modal listing the blocking vendors + their cutoff dates + a "Request admin override" CTA — no reason field, no vendor cards. Admin override is the only forward path. **In-flight protection:** changes initiated BEFORE the cutoff date and currently pending continue to run to completion even if the cutoff falls during the negotiation window; cutoffs apply to NEW attempts, not in-progress ones (otherwise mid-window cutoff transitions would auto-fail valid changes). **Retroactivity rule:** vendor edits to their cutoffs in 0022 do NOT apply retroactively to already-confirmed bookings. Cutoffs in effect at booking-confirmation time are snapshotted to `vendor_event_window.effective_date_cutoff` / `effective_venue_cutoff` / `effective_guest_count_cutoff` and become binding for that event. Prevents vendors from quietly shortening their acceptance window mid-engagement to block the couple. **Admin override (only forward path past cutoff):** couple files via 0029 help center → admin mediator reaches out to each past-cutoff vendor individually → if vendors agree, mediator applies the change via 0023 admin console; if vendors decline, the couple's only remaining option is to cancel that vendor's booking and find a replacement. Override actions log to `dispute_resolutions` with `cause = 'past_cutoff_override'`. **Out of scope:** cutoffs apply only to vendor bookings — Setnayan platform SKUs (Papic, Panood, Patiktok, LED, AI Highlights, etc.) have their own pre-event activation / rendering / fulfillment windows handled separately by the per-SKU flows. Locked into iteration 0021 § 13 (new section) with cross-references added to § 10, § 11, § 12. | Vendor margin protection: as the event approaches, vendors progressively commit inventory and crew — catering buys ingredients, florists buy flowers, lights & sound rosters crew, printers run name cards. Late-stage changes can be unrecoverable financial losses. The cutoff lets vendors declare upfront what proximity is too late for what kind of change, removing the negotiation tax (and the awkward "we have to say no" interaction) for changes that are physically impossible to accommodate at that proximity. **Defaults eliminate vendor configuration burden** for the common case — most catering vendors won't think to set a cutoff manually, but the V1 seed values match industry norms (caterers commonly lock headcount 14 days out; photographers can adapt to date changes within a week). **The retroactivity rule closes an adversarial edge case** — without it, a vendor unhappy with a potential reschedule could shorten their cutoff to 90 days the moment they sensed friction, instantly blocking the couple. Snapshotting cutoffs at booking confirmation means the terms that existed when the couple chose this vendor are the binding ones. **Admin override preserves couple agency** for genuine emergencies (typhoon, venue collapse, family crisis) while keeping the default flow tight and protecting vendors from frivolous late-stage requests. Affects iterations: 0021 (canonical spec, new § 13 + cross-refs in § 10/§ 11/§ 12), 0006 (vendor onboarding — three new cutoff fields with category-based defaults), 0022 (vendor dashboard — Change Acceptance Policy settings panel · NOT retroactive to confirmed bookings), 0023 (admin override workflow + `dispute_resolutions.cause = 'past_cutoff_override'`), 0029 (help center — past-cutoff override request form). |
| 2026-05-17 | **V1 SKU lock batch — Pricing & Frequency overhaul (Panood multi-cam always · max 6 cams · Papic reactivated · 6 Cam Bridge SKUs · 2 Panood Template Pack SKUs · Save-the-Date Video repriced · 2D billing model · Cost Watch primitive).** This row consolidates a multi-conversation pricing pivot into a single batched migration. **(1) Two-dimensional billing model added to `service_catalog`:** new `time_recurrence TEXT CHECK (one_time/weekly/quarterly/annual/lifetime)` + `event_scope TEXT CHECK (per_event/all_events)` columns. Maps owner's mental model exactly — "per day for single event" vs "annual for all events on the account" become distinct combinations rather than fighting in a single enum. `service_catalog_price_history` extended with prior/new columns for both new dimensions. **(2) Panood always-multi-cam pivot (max 6 cams enforced via Cloudflare Stream Live SFU `max_publishers: 6`):** `panood_daily_broadcast` repriced ₱499 → **₱2,499/day** (multi-cam baked in) · `panood_annual_streaming` repriced ₱2,999 → **₱19,999/year** with `time_recurrence=annual` + `event_scope=all_events` — explicitly positioned as a vendor / competition-organizer / multi-event subscription. **Retired same-day:** `panood_camera_sync_daily` (₱99/day) + `panood_annual_streaming_plus` (₱3,999/year) — both collapsed into the always-multi-cam pivot. **(3) Papic seats reactivated to V1** (previously deferred V1.5+ on 2026-05-16): `paparazzi_3_seats` ₱1,499 · `paparazzi_5_seats` ₱2,499 · `paparazzi_camera_addon` ₱999 (+1 seat add-on, multi-purchase). Reactivation justified because Papic capture is HTML/browser-based — no native-app gating per seat. Seat count is the "official paparazzi" UX limit the couple sets; guests can still upload via QR without consuming seats. Credits/album/book SKUs (`paparazzi_credits_addon` · `premium_guest_camera_pack` · `personal_album_per_guest` · `memory_book_per_guest`) stay V1.5+ deferred. **(4) Six product-scoped Cam Bridge SKUs (NEW · replaces the retired shared `pro_camera_bridge_seat`):** **Panood Cam Bridge** `panood_cam_bridge_slot_day` **₱199/slot/day** (single tier, premium per-slot pricing reflecting live-broadcast value); **Papic Cam Bridge** 3 tiers — `papic_cam_bridge_slot_day` **₱99/slot/day** · `papic_cam_bridge_all_slots_day` **₱249/day** (flat all-slots, breaks even vs per-slot at ≥3 DSLRs) · `papic_cam_bridge_all_slots_annual` **₱2,499/year** all-slots all-events (vendor subscription); **Patiktok Cam Bridge** 2 tiers — `patiktok_cam_bridge_day` **₱49/day** flat · `patiktok_cam_bridge_annual` **₱249/year** all-events. All 6 use the same underlying WiFi-SDK DSLR-pairing tech in the Papic-binary native app; pricing differs by product because perceived value of DSLR upgrade differs (Panood broadcast quality > Papic guest-photographer > Patiktok short-form). **(5) Two new Panood Template Pack SKUs (NEW):** `panood_template_pack_daily` **₱799/day** · `panood_template_pack_annual` **₱7,999/year all-events** — unlocks overlays + titles + transitions on the broadcast output (applies to both phone-cam AND Cam-Bridge-DSLR feeds equally). **(6) Save-the-Date Video repriced ₱99 → ₱199/render** with rationale anchored in the new Cost Watch primitive: highest observed render is ~₱45 (vision-heavy footage); at ₱99 the worst-case cost-to-price ratio is 45% (yellow); at ₱199 it drops to 23% (green). 2× margin capture at a price still under the ₱200 impulse-buy threshold; doesn't change the social-share end-card traffic play. **(7) `service_render_costs` table + `service_catalog_cost_watch` materialized view (NEW · Cost Watch feature):** every SKU consumption logs actual Setnayan-incurred cost + cost_breakdown JSONB (AI API + FFmpeg + storage + bandwidth + music license). The psychological anchor delta is negligible (-₱1 to -₱200 on prices ranging from ₱1,500 to ₱800,000 = 0.025% to 0.067% effective discount — invisible at the buyer's purchase moment) but the rule-consistency win is large (one canonical pricing convention across the platform, no "vendor B2B exception" carve-out needed). The annual revenue impact at projected 1,000 V1 vendor signups + assumed conversion mix is on the order of ~₱5K–₱50K/yr across all 8 SKUs combined — well within rounding noise of the ₱4M annual net projection from row 7. | 2026-05-18 | **Setnayan Concierge evolves into an AI-powered conversational planner — DIY-tier 3 free questions per event via free Cloudflare Workers AI (Llama 3.1 8B) + curated Filipino-wedding Brain · paid-tier `concierge_complete` ₱4,999/12mo upgrades synthesis to Claude Haiku 4.5 (reusing the 0032 Anthropic workspace) + adds event-data integration + 12-month conversation persistence + 9-step journey nudges · new top-level corpus folder `02_Specifications/18_Concierge_Brain/` (skeleton shipped today · content authoring is a parallel workstream).** First 2026-05-18 row. Owner-confirmed scope expansion (V1.5+) anchored on an existing shipped iteration (0016) rather than a new iteration, so plumbing reuse is heavy and net new schema is minimal. **(1) Architecture — locked at three tiers:** Tier 0 DIY = 3 free Q per event, stateless, generic-but-Filipino-fluent; Tier 1 Trial (existing 3-day card-less) = unlimited Q on free model, partial event-data; Tier 2 Concierge Complete = unlimited Q on Haiku 4.5, full event-data integration, persistent thread per event for 12 months, paid-tier-only brain chunks unlocked (honeymoon planning · 9-step deep-dive · priority vendor matching). **(2) Models — what runs where:** query embedding = Cloudflare Workers AI `bge-small-en-v1.5` (384-dim, free up to 10K neurons/day); vector search = Supabase pgvector `ivfflat` (free); synthesis DIY+Trial = `@cf/meta/llama-3.1-8b-instruct-fast` (free); synthesis Paid = `claude-haiku-4-5` via existing 0032 Anthropic workspace (~₱0.50/question, well within existing $500 cap). **(3) Free-tier sustainability math:** Cloudflare 10K neurons/day ≈ 1,500 synthesis requests/day at 8B-fast rates; projected V1 launch traffic ~100 new couples/day × 3 free Q = 300 Q/day = ~20% of daily quota; 5× growth headroom before paid-neuron spillover. **(4) Paid-tier cost ceiling:** ~₱25 inference cost per paid couple over 12 months vs ₱4,999 MRR equivalent = 0.5% of revenue. Negligible. **(5) Why 3 free questions specifically:** Q1 tests reality, Q2 is the first real question, Q3 produces the pattern moment where the AI references prior turns + surfaces specifically Filipino context + suggests deep-link CTAs — mental model lands at "this is useful" not "let me see if this is useful." Lower (1-2) doesn't earn conviction; higher (5+) cannibalizes trial conversion. **(6) Quota mechanics:** counter `events.concierge_free_questions_used INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (...BETWEEN 0 AND 3)`; increments only on successful LLM response (failures don't burn quota); reset only via admin action (`admin_reset_concierge_free_questions(event_id, admin_user_id, reason)`); per-event (not per-account) aligns with the existing `events.concierge_status` state machine — when V2 unlocks multi-event-per-couple, each event gets its own 3-Q allowance. **(7) Surface integration:** chat widget on dashboard Home (0021 § 2.0a area) — mobile bottom-sheet rising from a persistent "Ask the Concierge" pill in the thumb-zone; desktop right-side drawer 480px anchored to the Concierge journey block. DIY = collapsed CTA tile ("3 free questions") above the 10-tile grid that flips to the existing trial-upsell card after Q3. Trial + Active = always-available widget with sidebar chat history (paid only — DIY is stateless). **(8) Schema additions** (alongside existing 0016 schema): `events.concierge_free_questions_used` column with CHECK constraint; new tables `concierge_conversations` + `concierge_messages` (paid-tier history with `synthesis_model` + `cost_centavos` columns for cost watch) + `concierge_unanswered_questions` (feeds brain growth from real demand). Brain-chunk table (`concierge_brain_chunks` with `embedding VECTOR(384)` + `tier_visible_to TEXT[]` + `paid_tier_only BOOLEAN` + `is_active BOOLEAN`) + response cache table (`concierge_response_cache` with 24h TTL keyed on `query_hash`) spec'd in `18_Concierge_Brain/00_Architecture.md` § 5 — cross-iteration infra, sits under the brain folder rather than under 0016. **(9) Brain folder skeleton shipped today** (`02_Specifications/18_Concierge_Brain/`): README (overview · chunk authoring template · governance rules · tier-by-chunk visibility table · non-goals); `00_Architecture.md` (RAG pipeline · models · schema · server actions · failure-mode fallbacks · acceptance tests · V1.5+ deferred items); `01_Filipino_Cultural_Reference.md` (12 chunk stubs: pamamanhikan · despedida · ninang/ninong · candle/cord/veil · money dance · Catholic/Muslim/Civil tracks · combined ceremonies · engagement length · parental dance · PH photography customs); `02_Regional_Pricing_Benchmarks.md` (13 stubs: NCR · Tagaytay · Cebu · Davao · Boracay venues · per-head catering · photographer/videographer tiers · HMUA · gown · coordinator vs Concierge framing · peak vs off-peak · Saturday premium); `04_Planning_Timelines.md` (10 stubs paraphrasing 0016 § 1 in couple-language: 12/9/6/3/1-month + day-of + book-first dependency + marriage-license timeline + pre-Cana timeline + urgency triage); `05_Legal_BIR_Reference.md` (8 stubs cross-ref 0026 + 0034: marriage license · CENOMAR · Catholic parish docs · prenup basics · BIR 2307 · Setnayan Pay 5.0% fee disclosure · vendor contract red flags · RA 10173 guest privacy); `06_Setnayan_Feature_Reference.md` (15 stubs — every V1 iteration paraphrased + V1.5+ "coming soon" framing + the paid-tier-only Honeymoon Planning chunk + Daily Nudges chunk — drives upsell to Concierge); `07_Vendor_Decision_Logic.md` (12 stubs cross-ref 0006: book order · vendor comparison criteria per category · coordinator-tier decision · gown rental vs purchase · verification tiers · No-Convenience-Fee badge · payment milestone patterns · red flags · crew-meal expectations · negotiation timing · disputes); `08_Budget_Allocation_Reference.md` (11 stubs mirroring 0016 § 4–§ 5: 5 working-budget tiers · estimation formula · not-included categories · per-tier allocation tables · tier upgrade/downgrade signals · overspend traps · underspend traps · 5.0% Setnayan Pay in budget). **(10) Governance rules** (in brain README): single-admin authority for brain edits (mirrors 0006 review-gate appeal pattern · audit-logged in `admin_audit_log` with `target_table = 'concierge_brain_chunks'`); every chunk must cite source (UCCP / CBCP / DSWD / DOT / Setnayan internal data — no anonymous "common knowledge"); embedding hash invalidates on body edit (nightly sweep regenerates · manual "regenerate now" admin button available); paid-tier-only chunks tagged with `paid_tier_only = TRUE` and filtered server-side on retrieval. **(11) Admin surfaces (0023)** — three new tabs: Brain Editor (markdown editor + tag autocomplete + cross-ref linker + per-chunk re-embed button); Unanswered Questions queue (admin can author new chunks in response · drives brain growth from real demand); Concierge Cost Watch (per-tier and per-model spend aggregation · feeds existing `service_catalog_cost_watch` pattern). **(12) Free-tier exhaustion fallback** (when Cloudflare 10K neurons/day cap is hit across all couples): DIY responses fall back to top-1 retrieved chunk verbatim with capacity-reset footer ("Quick answer below — full personalized answer when daily capacity resets in the morning"). Paid tier falls through cleanly to Anthropic (separate provider · separate quota · no shared rate limit). **(13) Failure mode crossover with 0032** (Anthropic 5xx during Concierge Active request): degrades to Llama 8B + logs degradation event in `admin_audit_log`; **contract intelligence requests do NOT have this fallback** because contract analysis is synchronous and paid — couples paying for contract analysis get error toast + refund-eligible retry, not degraded fallback. **(14) Acceptance test additions** to 0016 `tests.md`: DIY Q1–Q3 success + Q4 upsell-card-no-LLM; DIY response stamped `synthesis_model = 'llama-3.1-8b'` vs Active stamped `'claude-haiku-4-5'`; Trial vs Active prompt difference verified (Trial = partial event-data, Active = full); Cloudflare 429 fallback to chunk-1 verbatim; Anthropic 5xx fallback to Llama with `admin_audit_log` row; `paid_tier_only = TRUE` chunks never retrieved for DIY/Trial; counter only increments on success; admin reset action audit-logged. **(15) Cross-iteration impact:** 0016 (canonical spec, new § 0a section); 0021 (upgrade banner gets contextual Q3 prefix); 0023 (three new admin tabs); 0029 (free-question-reset appeals route through existing help-ticket flow); 0032 (new § 4a cross-reference — shared Anthropic workspace + Haiku 4.5 model, no new spend cap needed); 0035 Observability (adds `concierge_*` event funnels: asked / responded / exhausted / trial_triggered / converted_to_paid to PostHog 3-event base set). **(16) V1.5+ deferred** (listed in `18_Concierge_Brain/00_Architecture.md` § 12): SSE streaming responses; voice input (Tagalog/English microphone); second-LLM verifier pass for hallucination check; multi-language synthesis (mirroring 0025 EN/TL/CEB locale toggle); 1-hour ephemeral memory for DIY sessions; honeymoon planning content depth. **(17) Sequencing note:** First 2026-05-18 row · evolves shipped 0016 rather than introducing new iteration · reuses 0032 Anthropic workspace + spend caps (no new external dependency) · brain content authoring is the long-pole content workstream (3–5 person-days of writing per the README, runs in parallel with engineering) · engineering work = ~12–15 person-days (brain pipeline · chat UI · quota enforcement · event-data integration · admin tabs · acceptance tests) · no V1 SKU changes (₱4,999/12mo Concierge Complete + 3-day trial preserved exactly) · no wallet UI introduced · responsive default honored (mobile bottom-sheet · desktop drawer) · PHP centavos pricing convention preserved (₱0.50/Q paid · ₱0.00 free) · `wedding` remains the only V1-selectable event type. | Three drivers. **First, competitive moat against discovery-only competitors** — knotch.studio analysis this session confirmed they ship pure rule-based filter+score with hand-curated tags marketed as "AI matching" (decompiled `_M(e)` = 4-rule weighted sum on hand-tagged supplier data, no embedding/LLM/vector search at runtime, zero `functions.invoke()` calls touching any AI provider). Setnayan can match their "AI feel" *and* go beyond into actual semantic matching + conversational planning at zero marginal cost via Cloudflare's free tier. The Filipino-wedding brain (pamamanhikan · ninang/ninong · regional pricing · BIR · seasonal weather · Setnayan-specific features) is the **structural moat** — no foreign-built planner (The Knot · Zola · Bridebook) can match it, and a local copycat needs to write the brain from scratch. **Second, evolves an existing paid SKU rather than creating new pricing surface area** — `concierge_complete` ₱4,999/12mo + 3-day trial mechanics are already locked (2026-05-17 PR); adding the AI brain *inside* that surface (without changing the price, SKU, or trial mechanic) makes the upgrade more valuable without restarting billing/cart engineering. The Q3 cliffhanger is the new freemium hook that funnels into the existing trial → paid conversion ladder. **Third, free-tier cost economics are airtight** — Cloudflare Workers AI 10K neurons/day free quota covers ~5× projected launch traffic on the 8B model; even paid Haiku 4.5 inference is ~0.5% of MRR per paid couple over 12 months. The 0032 Anthropic workspace + $500 hard cap already exists and has 8× headroom even when both Concierge Active and Contract Intelligence run through it. No new vendor signup, no new spend approval, no new external dependency to gate launch. **Flipside accepted:** small-model quality on DIY tier means some answers will be less polished than Haiku; mitigation is (a) the brain holds the substance so synthesis is paraphrase-not-reason — small models excel at paraphrase, (b) prompt enforces "use only retrieved context · if unsure say so" to bound hallucination, (c) the top-1 chunk verbatim fallback prevents hard failures on rate-limit days. **Risk: free is too good** — paid moat is event-data integration + persistence + nudges + paid-only chunks, not raw question quota. A couple asking generic "what's a Tagaytay budget for 150 pax?" gets a great free answer; a couple asking "given OUR 137-confirmed-guest list, OUR booked vendors, OUR budget, what's the next move?" only works on paid. Two qualitatively different products on the same engine. Affects iterations: **0016 (canonical, new § 0a)**, **0032 (new § 4a cross-reference)**, 0021 (upgrade banner Q3 prefix · existing surface), 0023 (three new admin tabs), 0029 (existing help-ticket flow extended), 0035 (PostHog funnels added); new corpus asset folder `02_Specifications/18_Concierge_Brain/` (README + 00_Architecture + 8 topic-cluster skeletons created today, content authoring pending). **`.docx` mirrors regenerated via pandoc** for 0016 and 0032 per established Cowork convention. **Engineering work pending**: brain pipeline (Cloudflare Worker for embedding · Supabase Edge Function for synthesis routing) · chat widget UI (mobile bottom-sheet · desktop drawer) · quota enforcement on `events.concierge_free_questions_used` · paid-tier event-data prompt assembler · three 0023 admin tabs · acceptance test fixtures. **Sequencing**: V1.5+ deferral — engineering starts after 0006 Phase 2 marketplace ships (real vendor data + service taxonomy needed to substantiate vendor-decision and budget-allocation brain chunks); brain content authoring can begin immediately in parallel. |

Both rows are accurate as of their respective wall-clock writes, but a future Claude reading them in sequence would see contradictory rationales without this realignment row. **Per the established "latest spec wins" discipline** (2026-05-17 lock per `2026-05-17_Session_Handoff_Pricing_and_Payments.md` § "Memory files"), row 2 architecture supersedes row 1 rationale; row 3 makes that explicit so neither prior row needs retroactive rewriting. **(4) Pricing-posture summary for next session:** Concierge Complete is a paid product even when its inference cost approaches zero, because the paid product is *the personalization layer*, not the LLM call. A couple paying ₱4,999 buys: chat that knows their specific event (guest list status · vendor ledger · budget tier · payment milestones) · persistent 12-month thread (Month-2 question recallable in Month-8) · daily proactive nudges per the 9-step journey · access to paid-tier-only chunks (honeymoon planning · priority vendor matching depth). A DIY couple paying ₱0 buys: 3 free questions per event answered by the same brain at generic-Filipino-context level (no event data · no persistence · no nudges · only non-paid-tier chunks). Two qualitatively different products on the same engine, with the launch promo widening the price gap for the first 10 calendar months (DIY effectively zero cost · paid pulls ahead via differentiation, not via lowered base). **(5) No code change required from this row:** `apps/web/lib/sku-catalog.ts` already correctly excludes `concierge_complete` from `LAUNCH_PROMO_SKU_CODES` (per row 1's app-side helpers). No migration needed (the SKU's `launch_promo_until` column stays NULL). No UI change (the `/pricing` + `/for-vendors` + `_TransparentPricing` § 8.5 surfaces shipped in row 1 already render Concierge as a paid SKU without a FREE badge). **(6) Affected files (corpus only):** `0016_step_by_step_plan_builder/0016_step_by_step_plan_builder.md` (new "Launch-promo exclusion" subsection inside § 0a between "Cross-iteration touchpoints" lead-in and the bullet list); `02_Specifications/18_Concierge_Brain/README.md` (new "Pricing posture — Concierge stays paid" section between "Non-goals" and end-of-file); `CLAUDE.md` (this row). **`.docx` mirror for 0016 regenerated via pandoc.** No other iteration .md edited. No engineering work added or removed from row 2's pending list. | Two drivers. **First, contradictory same-day rationale is a documentation bug that future sessions will trip on.** Row 1 said Concierge is excluded from the promo because it has "real coordinator labor"; row 2 said Concierge is AI-powered and the per-couple cost is ~₱25/12mo. Read together without this realignment, a future Claude could reasonably conclude "if the AI evolution drops the cost, why isn't Concierge in the promo?" and either propose adding it (wrong — would gut paid revenue) or propose unwinding the AI evolution (wrong — would lose the competitive moat against knotch-style discovery tools). Explicitly stating "the exclusion holds for a different reason now" closes that ambiguity. **Second, the paid moat needs to be framed correctly for the launch period.** The launch promo deliberately makes 16 zero-marginal-cost SKUs free to drive sign-ups and inventory growth (the 9 couple-side and 7 vendor-side promo'd SKUs all cost Setnayan nothing once built); Concierge is the only product Setnayan still charges for during the launch window aside from real-cost SKUs (AI Highlights · Monogram · Contract Intel · Verification · Ads). That positioning is intentional — Concierge is THE flagship paid product during the launch period, and the spec needs to be unambiguous about *why* it's worth paying for when adjacent products are free. The new framing — "paid value is personalization + persistence + nudges + paid-only chunks" — is also better marketing copy than "paid because coordinator labor costs us money" (which sounds like the price is a cost-pass-through rather than a value-anchored product). **Flipside accepted:** none material. Documentation reconciliation has no downside. The risk that an over-zealous future session might still try to fold Concierge into the promo is mitigated by row 1's explicit `LAUNCH_PROMO_SKU_CODES` enumeration (Concierge not present) and row 2's architecture lock (paid value is the personalization layer, which is purely a paid-tier feature). **Sequencing note:** Third 2026-05-18 row · realigns rows 1 and 2 of the same date · zero schema/code/SKU/price impact · purely documentation work · `.docx` mirror regenerated for 0016 only (brain README is in `02_Specifications/` which historically ships .md-only per the inventory at `Documents/Claude/Projects/Setnayan/02_Specifications/`); CLAUDE.md itself is meta-doc and follows the established "no .docx mirror" convention for the corpus root. |
| 2026-05-18 | **Contract Intelligence (iteration 0032) RETIRED. Replaced by free built-in dual e-signature on every vendor contract — vendor uploads PDF, both vendor and customer sign with canvas-captured signatures (PNG + IP + UA + signed_at), trigger seals the contract to `fully_signed` once both signatures land. No third-party e-sig vendor in V1. Notary EXPLICITLY EXCLUDED from V1 and V1.5 per owner — Philippine Notarial Law (2004) restricts a notary's commission to their RTC city/province, so in-house covers only ~10% of cross-city vendor-couple contracts and a partner network is out of scope. Couples who want notarization take the signed PDF to their own local notary.** Owner directive 2026-05-18. **Schema:** `vendor_contracts` table (contract_id, vendor_profile_id, event_id, order_id, uploaded_by_user_id, title, description, file_url, file_name, file_size_bytes ≤25MB, mime_type='application/pdf', status ∈ {draft, sent_for_signature, fully_signed, cancelled}, sent_for_signature_at, fully_signed_at, cancelled_at, cancelled_by_user_id, cancelled_reason, created_at, updated_at); `vendor_contract_signatures` table (signature_id, contract_id, signer_user_id, signer_role ∈ {vendor, customer}, signer_full_name, signature_image_url, signed_at, ip_address, user_agent, UNIQUE(contract_id, signer_role)). **Trigger:** `vendor_contract_check_fully_signed()` AFTER INSERT on signatures auto-flips the parent contract to `fully_signed` once both rows exist. **RLS:** vendor owns their contracts (matched via vendor_profiles.user_id); event members (couple+coordinator) read non-draft contracts on their event; admin has read for moderation; signers can INSERT only their own row on `sent_for_signature` contracts. **R2 bucket:** existing `setnayan-vendor-contracts` reused for both PDFs and signature PNGs (keyed `<vendor_profile_id>/<contract_id>/[signatures/{vendor,customer}.png|<filename>]`). **App surfaces:** `/vendor-dashboard/contracts` (list) → `/vendor-dashboard/contracts/new` (upload form gated to events the vendor has a chat thread with) → `/vendor-dashboard/contracts/[contractId]` (detail + send-for-sig + sign-as-vendor + cancel); `/dashboard/[eventId]/contracts` (list, RLS filters drafts) → `/dashboard/[eventId]/contracts/[contractId]` (detail + sign-as-customer). Shared client component `apps/web/app/_components/signature-canvas.tsx` (HTML5 pointer events, DPR-aware, emits PNG data URL into hidden input). Shared helper module `apps/web/lib/contracts.ts` (types, validators, formatters). **Signing-order policy:** either party can sign first once the contract is sent; the trigger seals when both rows exist. **SKU retirement:** `contract_intelligence_upgrade` (couple ₱199) + `contract_intelligence_per_contract` (vendor ₱199) flipped to is_active=FALSE in service_catalog; both added to `RETIRED_SKU_CODES` in `apps/web/lib/sku-catalog.ts`. `vendor_pro_weekly` description updated to remove the stale "free Contract Intelligence" perk and reference the new built-in e-signature flow instead. **Anthropic Console scope shrinks** but the workspace stays (Concierge brain paid tier still needs Haiku 4.5 per 2026-05-18 row 1). **V1.1 follow-ups noted inline (TODO comments):** PDF stamping (compositing signature images directly onto the PDF page rather than displaying them side-by-side), email notifications when a vendor sends a contract for signature, audit-trail export. | Owner directive 2026-05-18 — "remove the contract part completely. but still let the vendor upload a contract file in pdf. and we have the option to let them sign and the customer sign, and also offer legal notary for this. we will have an inhouse notary that can cater this? or that is not allowed since vendor may be from a different city?" Two reasons drove the simplification. **First, the AI-analysis SKU was paid scope (₱199/contract) that adds compliance + LLM-cost risk for marginal value** — every couple needs to upload-and-sign a vendor contract regardless of whether they want AI analysis, so making the underlying sign flow free + first-class is the more durable V1 surface. The Anthropic workspace + Haiku 4.5 spend cap stays because the paid-tier Concierge brain (2026-05-18 row 1) still routes through it; only the contract-analysis call paths drop out. **Second, Philippine Notarial Law makes in-house notary impractical** — the 2004 Rules on Notarial Practice commission a notary to a specific RTC city/province, and a document notarized outside that jurisdiction is void. Setnayan's wedding vendors and couples routinely span multiple cities (vendor in Quezon City, couple in Cebu, venue in Tagaytay), so an in-house notary in any one location covers maybe 10% of cross-city contracts. Partner-network notarization (NotaryNow.ph-style) is operationally viable but introduces a vendor + revenue-share + KYC layer that the owner explicitly excluded from V1 AND V1.5. The fallback — couples taking the signed PDF to their own local notary if they want notarization — is the simplest path and stays out of Setnayan's compliance surface. **Affects:** new migration `20260518200000_vendor_contracts_dual_esign_retire_0032.sql` (idempotent · vendor_contracts + signatures + retire 2 SKUs + update vendor_pro_weekly description); new `apps/web/lib/contracts.ts` (helpers/types); new `apps/web/app/_components/signature-canvas.tsx` (client component); new server actions at `apps/web/app/vendor-dashboard/contracts/actions.ts` + `apps/web/app/dashboard/[eventId]/contracts/actions.ts`; new pages at `/vendor-dashboard/contracts/{,new,[contractId]}` + `/dashboard/[eventId]/contracts/{,[contractId]}`; `apps/web/lib/sku-catalog.ts` (mark `contract_intelligence_per_contract` is_active=false + extend RETIRED_SKU_CODES with both SKUs); `apps/web/app/vendor-dashboard/layout.tsx` (add Contracts subnav tab between Bookings and Messages). **Iteration 0032 spec status flip to "retired 2026-05-18"** in `App_Build_Status.md` is pending (next Cowork pass). **`.docx` mirror NOT regenerated** (pandoc availability unverified in this session — flag for future Cowork pass). |

DIY = unrestricted basic vendor search; Concierge = active conducted flow: intake forms → foundation-first (church OR venue lock first, then nearby alternates within 30km · vendors within 8km per 0016 § 2) → vendor recommendations (saturation-aware) → tracking/Next Actions surface → vendor share packs. Deterministic state machine + templates; LLM only fires when couple types a free-form question. Q&A responses cached by `hash(query_embedding, chunk_ids, combination_bucket)` with **NO TTL** (supersedes the 24h TTL in `18_Concierge_Brain/00_Architecture.md` § 5) — invalidated only when underlying brain chunks change (lazy regenerate on next access). | 2026-05-18 | **V1 launches as personal/family pilot first — DTI-gated chain deferred · payments stay live for unlock/lock testing · Supabase migrations confirmed in-sync.** Eighth 2026-05-18 row. Owner strategic posture lock: rather than a public V1 launch, Setnayan ships first to a closed 5-20 person personal/family cohort to exercise the FULL cart → checkout → activation → expiry → cancellation cycle against real (small-scale) flows. Defers every DTI-gated downstream item until pilot wraps; keeps payments live (not a no-payment pilot) so the unlock/lock state machine gets real coverage before public traffic. **(1) Pilot scope — closed 5-20 people, full payment cycle.** Cohort = owner's immediate family + close friends (Filipino weddings + adjacent events the owner can personally support). Pilot exercises the entire money-touching surface: real cart additions, real Setnayan Pay 5% fee captures, real `service_catalog.launch_promo_until` ₱0 SKUs vs paid SKUs, real `concierge_complete` ₱2,499 24mo windows, real activation timestamps, real expiry sweeps, real cancellation refund paths. Owner explicitly chose **payments-on** (not a no-payment dry-run) because the lock/unlock state transitions are the single biggest source of latent V1 bugs and the only way to exercise them honestly is with real charge events flowing through the manual-reconciliation admin queue. **(2) DTI-gated chain DEFERRED in full until pilot wraps:** DTI Business Name registration · BIR Form 2303 (Certificate of Registration) · Mayor's Permit (Quezon City) · BDO Business Account · GCash Business Account · Maya Business Account · TikTok Business Verification · Camera SDK access (Sony/Canon/Nikon Pro programs all require registered business entity). These are all downstream of DTI — without the Certificate of Business Name Registration as the upstream anchor, none of the other applications can be filed. Punch-list items #17b · #17c · #17d · #17e · #17f and the Camera SDK lane in `API_Integration_Checklist.md` stay 🔴 not-started; status flips to 🟡 in-progress AFTER pilot wraps and the owner files DTI as Step 1 of the formal launch chain. **(3) DPO appointment + NPC registration STILL proceeds during pilot** — RA 10173 (Data Privacy Act of 2012) applies to any personal-information controller regardless of revenue or registration status, so DPO appointment cannot be deferred even though the pilot is small. Owner appoints themselves as DPO (no contracted DPO cost) — the "contracted-out DPO" option from punch-list #18 is deferred until post-pilot when the formal entity is in place. NPC registration (Sec. 5 of the IRR) still happens during pilot under the owner's personal name as PIC until DTI gives Setnayan a registered trade name. **(4) Anthropic Console signup MAY be deferred if Concierge AI brain is hidden during pilot** — the wizard flow can run on free Cloudflare Workers AI (Llama 3.1 8B) for the 5-20 cohort if owner chooses to hide the paid-tier Haiku 4.5 synthesis layer; in that case Anthropic Console signup drops off the pre-pilot punch list and rejoins the post-pilot ramp. **If Concierge is exposed in pilot** (owner wants to dogfood the paid surface with real family weddings), Anthropic Console signup + spend cap configuration + workspace setup stays on the punch list per 0032 § 4 (now § 4a after 2026-05-18 contract-intel retirement) — that decision is owner's call before pilot launch day. **(5) Currently in flight from today's session:** **Google OAuth Phase 1 ✅ done** (project · YouTube Data API v3 · OAuth consent screen · Client ID/Secret all provisioned — captured in row 6 of today, no separate row needed); **Phase 2 verified-app submission still pending** the three parallel prereqs from row 6 — privacy policy disclosure ✅ merged in PR #116, engineering OAuth handshake (callback route + `oauth_grants` schema extension + popup flow) still pending engineering, demo video recording still pending owner.
